_________________ test_the_question_lapses_after_eight_seconds _________________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ad6b620>

    def test_the_question_lapses_after_eight_seconds(env: Any) -> None:
        rig, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.clock.install()
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            _arm(page)
            page.clock.pause_at(
                page.evaluate("(Date.now() + 500) / 1000")
            )  # epoch seconds  # from here only the test moves time: no slow-runner drift
            page.clock.run_for(5000)
            assert page.inner_text(MOVE) == ASK  # still asked at about 5.5 seconds
            page.clock.run_for(3000)
            assert page.inner_text(MOVE) == "Move" and _step(rig, lid) == "new"  # lapsed after 8: a click asks again
    
>       _browse(base, body)

    def test_a_question_asked_for_one_lead_does_not_survive_a_look_at_another_lead(env: Any) -> None:
        rig, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
        other = str(rig.desk.process("form", "c-other", lead(email="other@example.test")).body["lead_id"])
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            _arm(page)
            show = '(id) => document.querySelector(`[data-lead-id="${id}"]`).click()'  # the panel covers the list
            page.evaluate(show, other)  # the panel is replaced, not closed
            page.wait_for_function(
                '() => document.querySelector(\'[data-testid="lead-drawer"] [data-testid="step-select"]\')'
            )
            page.wait_for_timeout(600)  # past the double-click gap
            page.evaluate(show, lid)
            page.wait_for_function("() => document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"step-select\"]')?.value === ''")
            page.wait_for_timeout(600)
            assert page.input_value(SELECT) == "" and page.inner_text(MOVE) == "Move"  # asks from the start again
            page.select_option(SELECT, "replied")
            page.click(MOVE)
            assert page.inner_text(MOVE) == ASK and _step(rig, lid) == "new"  # one click only asks

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ad696a0>

    def test_arming_moves_nothing_and_says_how_long_the_question_lasts(env: Any) -> None:
        rig, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            page.select_option(SELECT, "replied")
            page.wait_for_timeout(700)  # the panel slides in: measure when it has stopped
            find = (
                '() => { const r = s => document.querySelector(`[data-testid="${s}"]`).getBoundingClientRect();'
                " const c = document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"step-control\"]').getBoundingClientRect();"
                " return [r('step-move').top, r('step-hint').top, r('step-move').bottom, r('step-hint').height,"
                " c.height, c.bottom]; }"
            )
            before = page.evaluate(find)
            assert page.inner_text('[data-testid="step-hint"]') == "A lead only moves forward."
            assert before[1] >= before[2]  # its own row: it starts below the bottom of the Move button
            page.click(MOVE)
            page.wait_for_timeout(400)
            assert page.inner_text('[data-testid="step-hint"]') == "Click again within 8 s."
            assert page.evaluate(find) == before  # nothing moved, nothing grew

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_ test_the_confirm_button_has_readable_text_and_stands_out_from_the_note[light] _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ade6480>, scheme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_the_confirm_button_has_readable_text_and_stands_out_from_the_note(env: Any, scheme: str) -> None:
        rig, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            _arm(page)
            page.wait_for_timeout(400)  # the colour changes with a short transition
            got = page.evaluate(
                """() => { const rgb = e => getComputedStyle(e).backgroundColor;
                  const b = document.querySelector('[data-testid="step-move"]');
                  return { fill: rgb(b), ink: getComputedStyle(b).color,
                           edge: getComputedStyle(b).borderTopColor,
                           note: rgb(document.querySelector('[data-testid="step-crm-warning"]')) }; }"""
            )
    
            def parse(c: str) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
                nums = [
                    float(x) for x in c[c.index("(") + 1 : c.index(")")].replace("/", ",").replace(" ", ",").split(",") if x
                ]
                assert len(nums) == 3 or nums[3] == 1, c  # opaque colours only: a blend would need the page behind it
                return nums[0], nums[1], nums[2]
    
            fill, ink, note, edge = (parse(got[k]) for k in ("fill", "ink", "note", "edge"))
            assert _ratio(ink, fill) >= 4.5, got  # the words on the button
            assert _lum(ink) < _lum(fill), got  # dark words on a bright amber fill in both themes, one look
            assert max(_ratio(fill, note), _ratio(edge, note)) >= 3, (
                got
            )  # the button (its fill or its edge) against the note above it
    
>       _browse(base, body, scheme=scheme)

_ test_the_warning_is_fully_in_view_in_the_panel_and_the_confirm_button_is_amber[1100-light] _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ade6c90>, width = 1100, scheme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1440])
    def test_the_warning_is_fully_in_view_in_the_panel_and_the_confirm_button_is_amber(
        env: Any, width: int, scheme: str
    ) -> None:
        rig, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            seen = page.evaluate(
                """() => { const w = document.querySelector('[data-testid="step-crm-warning"]').getBoundingClientRect();
                  const b = document.querySelector('.drawer-body').getBoundingClientRect();
                  const m = document.querySelector('[data-testid="step-move"]').getBoundingClientRect();
                  return { wTop: w.top, wBottom: w.bottom, bTop: b.top, bBottom: b.bottom, mBottom: m.bottom,
                           mTop: m.top, scroll: document.querySelector('.drawer-body').scrollTop }; }"""
            )
            assert seen["scroll"] == 0 and seen["bTop"] <= seen["wTop"] and seen["wBottom"] <= seen["bBottom"], seen
            assert seen["wBottom"] <= seen["mTop"], seen  # above the choice, not below it
            assert seen["mBottom"] <= seen["bBottom"], seen  # and the Move row is in view too
            plain = page.eval_on_selector(MOVE, "e => getComputedStyle(e).backgroundColor")
            _arm(page)
            page.wait_for_timeout(400)  # the button's colour changes with a short transition
            asked = page.eval_on_selector(MOVE, "e => getComputedStyle(e).backgroundColor")

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1440])
    def test_the_warning_is_fully_in_view_in_the_panel_and_the_confirm_button_is_amber(
        env: Any, width: int, scheme: str
    ) -> None:

        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            seen = page.evaluate(
                """() => { const w = document.querySelector('[data-testid="step-crm-warning"]').getBoundingClientRect();
                  const b = document.querySelector('.drawer-body').getBoundingClientRect();
                  const m = document.querySelector('[data-testid="step-move"]').getBoundingClientRect();
                  return { wTop: w.top, wBottom: w.bottom, bTop: b.top, bBottom: b.bottom, mBottom: m.bottom,
                           mTop: m.top, scroll: document.querySelector('.drawer-body').scrollTop }; }"""
            )

            assert seen["wBottom"] <= seen["mTop"], seen  # above the choice, not below it
            assert seen["mBottom"] <= seen["bBottom"], seen  # and the Move row is in view too

_ test_the_note_after_a_give_up_promises_only_what_the_lead_can_do[closed-The desk gave up after several tries. A lead already at the last stage is not copied again.] _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4add7080>, to = 'closed'
text = 'The desk gave up after several tries. A lead already at the last stage is not copied again.'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(("to", "text"), [("closed", GAVE_UP + LAST), ("contacted", GAVE_UP + LATER)])
    def test_the_note_after_a_give_up_promises_only_what_the_lead_can_do(env: Any, to: str, text: str) -> None:
        import sqlite3
    
        from lead_desk.constants import MIRROR_MAX_TRIES
    
        rig, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
        if to == "closed":
            assert rig.desk.set_step(lid, "closed").status == 200
        con = sqlite3.connect(rig.db_path)
        con.execute("UPDATE lead_state SET crm_tries=? WHERE lead_id=?", (MIRROR_MAX_TRIES, lid))
        con.execute("UPDATE followups SET crm_done=1 WHERE lead_id=?", (lid,))
        con.commit()
        con.close()
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid) if to != "closed" else _open_closed(page, base, lid)
            note = page.locator('[data-testid="proof-ledger"] .chip-with-note .step-note').inner_text()
            assert note == text, note
    
>       _browse(base, body)

________ test_a_long_name_stays_inside_the_confirm_question[1280-light] ________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ad8c9e0>, scheme = 'light', width = 1280

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", SCHEMES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", WIDTHS)
    def test_a_long_name_stays_inside_the_confirm_question(env: Any, scheme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.add_init_script("window.sessionStorage.setItem('lead-desk-hide-personal', '0')")  # names shown
            _open_needs(page, base)
            _ask(page)
            assert LONG in page.inner_text('[data-testid="confirm-dialog"] p')
            m = page.evaluate(ESCAPES, '[data-testid="confirm-dialog"]')
            assert not m["out"], m  # text and buttons lie inside the dialog box and the window
            d = page.evaluate("""() => { const e = document.querySelector('[data-testid="confirm-dialog"]');
              return [e.scrollWidth, e.clientWidth]; }""")
            assert d[0] <= d[1] + 1, d
            page.click('[data-testid="confirm-ok"]')  # the buttons can be pressed
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="confirm-dialog"]', state="detached")
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=scheme)

            m = page.evaluate(ESCAPES, '[data-testid="confirm-dialog"]')
            assert not m["out"], m  # text and buttons lie inside the dialog box and the window

              return [e.scrollWidth, e.clientWidth]; }""")
            assert d[0] <= d[1] + 1, d
            page.click('[data-testid="confirm-ok"]')  # the buttons can be pressed
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="confirm-dialog"]', state="detached")

___ test_a_long_desk_note_stays_inside_the_message_at_the_corner[1280-light] ___

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4adcefc0>, scheme = 'light', width = 1280

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", SCHEMES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", WIDTHS)
    def test_a_long_desk_note_stays_inside_the_message_at_the_corner(env: Any, scheme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_needs(page, base)
            page.route(
                f"**/review/{UNKNOWN}/resolve",
                lambda r: r.fulfill(status=409, content_type="application/json", body=json.dumps({"hint": LONG})),
            )
            _ask(page)
            page.click('[data-testid="confirm-ok"]')
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="toast"]')
            assert LONG in page.inner_text('[data-testid="toast"]')
            m = page.evaluate(ESCAPES, '[data-testid="toast"]')
            assert not m["out"], m  # the text and its close button lie inside the message and the window
            x = page.evaluate("document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"toast\"] .toast-x').getBoundingClientRect().width")
            assert x >= 23, x  # the close button keeps its size
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=scheme)

_____ test_long_values_stay_inside_their_needs_a_person_cards[1280-light] ______

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4adcd910>, scheme = 'light', width = 1280

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", SCHEMES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", WIDTHS)
    def test_long_values_stay_inside_their_needs_a_person_cards(env: Any, scheme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.add_init_script("window.sessionStorage.setItem('lead-desk-hide-personal', '0')")
            for tab, item in (("action", UNKNOWN), ("action", "R13"), ("info", "D2")):
                _open_needs(page, base)
                if tab != "action":
                    page.click(f'[data-testid="needs-tab-{tab}"]')
                card = f'[data-item-id="{item}"]'
                page.wait_for_selector(card)
                m = page.evaluate(ESCAPES, card)
                assert not m["out"], (item, m)  # name, heading, reason, notes and desk note stay inside the card
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=scheme)

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ada0f50>, scheme = 'dark', width = 390

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", SCHEMES)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", WIDTHS)
    def test_long_values_stay_inside_their_needs_a_person_cards(env: Any, scheme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.add_init_script("window.sessionStorage.setItem('lead-desk-hide-personal', '0')")
            for tab, item in (("action", UNKNOWN), ("action", "R13"), ("info", "D2")):
                _open_needs(page, base)
                if tab != "action":
                    page.click(f'[data-testid="needs-tab-{tab}"]')
                card = f'[data-item-id="{item}"]'
                page.wait_for_selector(card)
                m = page.evaluate(ESCAPES, card)
                assert not m["out"], (item, m)  # name, heading, reason, notes and desk note stay inside the card
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=scheme)

_________ test_a_long_company_does_not_squeeze_the_leads_table[light] __________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ada0ad0>, scheme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", SCHEMES)
    def test_a_long_company_does_not_squeeze_the_leads_table(env: Any, scheme: str) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _new(rig, "ok-1", "Wenda Brook", "wenda@example.test")
        assert rig.desk.process("form", "long-1", lead(name=LONG, company=LONG, email="long@example.test")).status == 201
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            page.locator('[data-testid="leads-hide-personal"]').uncheck()
            m = page.evaluate("""() => { const w = document.querySelector('[data-testid="leads-table"]');
              const name = w.querySelector('tbody tr td:first-child').getBoundingClientRect().width;
              return { scroll: w.scrollWidth, client: w.clientWidth, name }; }""")
            assert m["scroll"] <= m["client"] + 1, m  # the table needs no sideways scroll for one long company
            assert m["name"] >= 120, m  # and the name column keeps a readable width
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=1280, scheme=scheme)

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_ test_one_colour_per_follow_up_state_in_ring_legend_timeline_and_badge[light] _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49bc76b0>, theme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", THEMES)
    def test_one_colour_per_follow_up_state_in_ring_legend_timeline_and_badge(env, theme: str) -> None:
        rig, base = fresh(env)
        first = _seed(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _serve_json(page, "**/api/ui/summary*", _summary_with_every_state())
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="followups-bar"] .legend li')
            _theme(page, theme)
            ring = page.evaluate(
                """() => {
                  const arcs = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=followups-bar] circle:not(.arc-track)')]
                    .map((c) => getComputedStyle(c).stroke);
                  const legend = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=followups-bar] .legend li')].map((li) => ({
                    label: li.querySelector('.legend-label').textContent,
                    dot: getComputedStyle(li.querySelector('.sw')).backgroundColor,
                    tone: [...li.querySelector('.sw').classList].find((c) => c.startsWith('sw-') && c !== 'sw')
                      .slice(3) }));
                  return { arcs, legend }; }"""
            )
            names = {
                "Sent": "sent",
                "Queued": "pending",
                "Sending": "sending",
                "Failed": "failed",
                "Did not finish": "did_not_finish",
                "Outcome unknown": "unknown",
                "CRM not confirmed": "crm_unconfirmed",
            }
            legend = {names[x["label"]]: x for x in ring["legend"]}
            assert sorted(legend) == sorted(STATES), ring["legend"]
            arc = {names[x["label"]]: ring["arcs"][i] for i, x in enumerate(ring["legend"])}
            for s in STATES:  # the ring slice and its legend dot are the same colour
                assert arc[s].replace("rgb", "") == legend[s]["dot"].replace("rgb", ""), (s, arc[s], legend[s])
            assert len({legend[s]["dot"] for s in STATES}) == len(STATES), "two states share one colour"
    
            badge = page.evaluate(
                """(states) => states.map((s) => { const el = window.Portal.ui.pill(s); document.body.appendChild(el);
                  const cs = getComputedStyle(el); const r = { s, bg: cs.backgroundColor, cls: el.className };
                  el.remove(); return r; })""",
                STATES,
            )
            for b in badge:  # the badge carries the tone of its state, and no two states share a badge colour
                assert "pill-" + legend[b["s"]]["tone"] in b["cls"].split(), (b, legend[b["s"]])
            assert len({b["bg"] for b in badge}) == len(STATES), badge
    
            dots = {}
            for s in STATES:
                _serve_json(page, f"**/api/ui/leads/{first}*", _detail_in_state(s))
                page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
                page.wait_for_selector(f'[data-lead-id="{first}"]')
                page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{first}"]')
                _theme(page, theme)
                page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="timeline"] li')
                dots[s] = page.evaluate(
                    """() => { const li = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=timeline] li')].pop();
                      return getComputedStyle(li, '::before').backgroundColor; }"""
                )
                page.reload()
                page.unroute(f"**/api/ui/leads/{first}*")
            for s in STATES:
                assert dots[s] == legend[s]["dot"], (s, dots[s], legend[s]["dot"])
    
>       _run(base, body)

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ada0ec0>, theme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", THEMES)
    def test_ring_slices_have_a_visible_gap(env, theme: str) -> None:
        rig, base = fresh(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _serve_json(page, "**/api/ui/summary*", _summary_with_every_state())
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="followups-bar"] circle')
            _theme(page, theme)
            arcs = page.evaluate(
                """() => [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=followups-bar] circle:not(.arc-track)')].map((c) => ({
                  dash: parseFloat(c.getAttribute('stroke-dasharray')),
                  start: -parseFloat(c.getAttribute('stroke-dashoffset')) }))"""
            )
            assert len(arcs) == len(STATES)
            for a, b in zip(arcs, arcs[1:], strict=False):
                assert b["start"] - (a["start"] + a["dash"]) >= 1.9, (a, b)  # out of 100 round the ring
    
>       _run(base, body)

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
__ test_needs_cards_of_one_row_are_equally_high_and_none_is_left_alone[1280] ___

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49b21fa0>, width = 1280

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1280, 1920])
    def test_needs_cards_of_one_row_are_equally_high_and_none_is_left_alone(env, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = fresh(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            review = _json("review.json")
            review["items"] = [i for i in review["items"] if i["tab"] == "action"]
            assert len(review["items"]) % 2 == 1  # an odd count: one card is last on its own
            _serve_json(page, "**/api/ui/review*", review)
            page.set_viewport_size({"width": width, "height": 1000})
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/needs")
            page.wait_for_selector(".need")
            page.wait_for_timeout(500)
            rows = _rows(page, ".needs-list > .need")
            for top, heights in rows.items():
                assert max(heights) - min(heights) <= 1, (top, heights)
            wide = page.evaluate(
                """() => { const l = document.querySelector('.needs-list').getBoundingClientRect();
                  const last = [...document.querySelectorAll('.needs-list > .need')].pop().getBoundingClientRect();
                  return [Math.round(l.width), Math.round(last.width)]; }"""
            )
            assert abs(wide[0] - wide[1]) <= 1, wide  # the last, odd card takes the whole row
    
>       _run(base, body)

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_____________ test_hidden_words_are_not_visible_and_card_lines_are _____________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4adcd5b0>

    def test_hidden_words_are_not_visible_and_card_lines_are(env) -> None:
        rig, base = fresh(env)
        first = _seed(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open(page, base, first)
            got = page.evaluate(
                """(re) => { const bad = [], rx = new RegExp(re);
                  const w = document.createTreeWalker(document.body, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT); let n;
                  while ((n = w.nextNode())) {
                    if (!rx.test(n.nodeValue)) continue;
                    const r = n.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
                    if (r.width > 2 && r.height > 2) bad.push(n.nodeValue);
                  }
                  const meta = [...document.querySelectorAll('.later-meta')]
                    .map((e) => Math.round(e.getBoundingClientRect().width));
                  const cards = document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=later-card]').length;
                  const spans = [...document.querySelectorAll('.sr-only')].length;
                  return { bad, meta, cards, spans }; }""",
                HIDDEN_WORDS,
            )
            assert got["bad"] == [] and got["spans"] > 0
            assert got["cards"] == 1 and len(got["meta"]) == 2 and min(got["meta"]) > 50  # both lines of the card are shown
            assert "1 minute after the first message" in page.inner_text('[data-testid="later-card"]')
    
>       _run(base, body)

_______________ test_no_attribute_or_title_holds_a_masked_value ________________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4b2b1e50>

    def test_no_attribute_or_title_holds_a_masked_value(env) -> None:
        rig, base = fresh(env)
        first = _seed(rig)
        rig.desk.handle("webhook", REF, lead(name="Ref Person", email="ref@example.test"))
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            for route, setup in (
                ("leads", lambda: _open(page, base, first)),
                ("log", None),
                ("overview", None),
                ("data", None),
            ):
                if setup:
                    setup()
                else:
                    page.goto(base + "/ui/#/" + route)
                    page.wait_for_selector("main, #page-body")
                    time.sleep(0.6)
                blob = page.evaluate(
                    """() => { const out = [document.title];
                      for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('*')) for (const a of el.attributes) out.push(a.value);
                      for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('input')) out.push(el.value);
                      return out.join('\\n'); }"""
                ).lower()
                for secret in (LOCAL, PHONE_NEW.lower(), MESSAGE, "ref-canary"):
                    assert secret.lower() not in blob, (route, secret)
    
>       _run(base, body)

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49bc4d10>, width = 1100

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1280])
    def test_the_hide_switch_and_the_table_keep_their_place_in_both_states(env, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = fresh(env)
        _seed(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.set_viewport_size({"width": width, "height": 800})
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            spots = []
            for on in (True, False):
                _flip(page, on)
                page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
                time.sleep(0.4)
                spots.append(
                    page.evaluate(
                        """() => { const r = (s) => { const b = document.querySelector(s).getBoundingClientRect();
                          return [Math.round(b.left), Math.round(b.top)]; };
                          return [r('.hide-switch'), r('[data-testid=leads-table]')]; }"""
                    )
                )
            assert spots[0] == spots[1], spots
    
>       _run(base, body)

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
____ test_no_sideways_scroll_revealed_leads_search_and_drawers[light-1100] _____

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49bc23c0>, theme = 'light', width = 1100

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1366])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_no_sideways_scroll_revealed_leads_search_and_drawers(env: Any, theme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        ids = _seed_long(rig)
        init = "window.sessionStorage.setItem('lead-desk-hide-personal', '0')"
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            _set(page, theme, "0")
            page.wait_for_timeout(300)
            assert (w := _no_sideways(page))[0] <= w[1], ("leads", w)
            heads = page.evaluate(
                "() => Object.fromEntries([...document.querySelectorAll('.leads-out thead th')]"
                ".map(x => [x.textContent.trim(), getComputedStyle(x).display]))"
            )
            hidden = "none" if width <= 1300 else "table-cell"  # both fall away only up to 1300 px: the room is used
            assert heads["Requests"] == hidden and heads["Source"] == hidden, heads
            page.fill('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "Alexandria")
            page.wait_for_timeout(700)
            assert (w := _no_sideways(page))[0] <= w[1], ("search", w)
            page.fill('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "")
            page.wait_for_timeout(700)
            for lid in ids:
                page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{lid}"]')
                page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="lead-drawer"]')
                page.wait_for_timeout(500)
                assert (w := _no_sideways(page))[0] <= w[1], ("drawer", lid, w)
                page.keyboard.press("Escape")
                page.wait_for_timeout(300)
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, init=init, scheme=theme)

        init = "window.sessionStorage.setItem('lead-desk-hide-personal', '0')"
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            _set(page, theme, "0")
            page.wait_for_timeout(300)
            assert (w := _no_sideways(page))[0] <= w[1], ("leads", w)
            heads = page.evaluate(
                "() => Object.fromEntries([...document.querySelectorAll('.leads-out thead th')]"
                ".map(x => [x.textContent.trim(), getComputedStyle(x).display]))"
            )
            hidden = "none" if width <= 1300 else "table-cell"  # both fall away only up to 1300 px: the room is used
            assert heads["Requests"] == hidden and heads["Source"] == hidden, heads
            page.fill('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "Alexandria")
            page.wait_for_timeout(700)
            assert (w := _no_sideways(page))[0] <= w[1], ("search", w)
            page.fill('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "")
            page.wait_for_timeout(700)
            for lid in ids:
                page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{lid}"]')
                page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="lead-drawer"]')
                page.wait_for_timeout(500)
                assert (w := _no_sideways(page))[0] <= w[1], ("drawer", lid, w)
                page.keyboard.press("Escape")
                page.wait_for_timeout(300)
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, init=init, scheme=theme)

__________ test_masked_email_and_last_change_stay_on_one_line[light] ___________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49bc47a0>, theme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_masked_email_and_last_change_stay_on_one_line(env: Any, theme: str) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_long(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            _set(page, theme, "1")
            got = page.evaluate(
                """() => { const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=lead-row]')];
                  const h = (e) => Math.round(e.getBoundingClientRect().height);
                  const mail = rows.map(r => r.querySelector('.lead-mail')).filter(Boolean).map(h);
                  const last = rows.map(r => r.querySelector('td:nth-last-child(1) time, td:nth-last-child(1)')).map(h);
                  return { mail, last, line: parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.body).lineHeight) }; }"""
            )
            assert got["mail"] and max(got["mail"]) <= got["line"] * 1.6, got  # one line, not three
            assert max(got["last"]) <= got["line"] * 2.6 + 24, got
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=1100, scheme=theme)

_________________ test_overview_proof_strip_layout[1100-light] _________________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49bc3d10>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x7f0d49bc2930>
theme = 'light', width = 1100

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1280, 1920])
    def test_overview_proof_strip_layout(env: Any, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, theme: str, width: int) -> None:
        monkeypatch.setattr(server, "load_demo_portal", lambda desk: _DemoStub())
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_long(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="proof"]')
            _set(page, theme, "1")
            time.sleep(0.4)
            got = page.evaluate(
                """() => { const p = document.querySelector('[data-testid=proof]');
                  const box = (e) => { const r = e.getBoundingClientRect(); return [r.left, r.top, r.right, r.bottom]; };
                  const figs = [...p.querySelectorAll('.dm-figure')].map(box);
                  const labels = [...p.querySelectorAll('.dm-figure-label')].map(e => e.getBoundingClientRect().height);
                  const pill = p.querySelector('.pill'); const lh = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(p).lineHeight) || 20;
                  return { p: box(p), figs, labels, pill: pill ? box(pill) : null, h: p.getBoundingClientRect().height,
                           w: [document.documentElement.scrollWidth, document.documentElement.clientWidth], lh }; }"""
            )
            shot = os.environ.get("UI4_SHOT_DIR")
            if shot:
                page.screenshot(path=f"{shot}/proof-{theme}-{width}.png")
            assert got["w"][0] <= got["w"][1], got
            assert len(got["figs"]) == 2 and got["pill"], got
    
            def hit(a: list[float], b: list[float]) -> bool:
                return a[0] < b[2] and b[0] < a[2] and a[1] < b[3] and b[1] < a[3]
    
            if got["pill"]:
                assert not any(hit(got["pill"], f) for f in got["figs"]), got  # the pill never covers a figure
            assert max(got["labels"]) <= 3 * 20, got  # a label is at most 3 lines, not one word per line
            if width >= 1280:
                assert got["h"] <= 170, got  # one calm row, not a 230 px tall block
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=theme)

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_________________ test_overview_proof_strip_layout[1280-dark] __________________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49b1a900>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x7f0d4adecd40>
theme = 'dark', width = 1280

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1280, 1920])
    def test_overview_proof_strip_layout(env: Any, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, theme: str, width: int) -> None:
        monkeypatch.setattr(server, "load_demo_portal", lambda desk: _DemoStub())
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_long(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="proof"]')
            _set(page, theme, "1")
            time.sleep(0.4)
            got = page.evaluate(
                """() => { const p = document.querySelector('[data-testid=proof]');
                  const box = (e) => { const r = e.getBoundingClientRect(); return [r.left, r.top, r.right, r.bottom]; };
                  const figs = [...p.querySelectorAll('.dm-figure')].map(box);
                  const labels = [...p.querySelectorAll('.dm-figure-label')].map(e => e.getBoundingClientRect().height);
                  const pill = p.querySelector('.pill'); const lh = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(p).lineHeight) || 20;
                  return { p: box(p), figs, labels, pill: pill ? box(pill) : null, h: p.getBoundingClientRect().height,
                           w: [document.documentElement.scrollWidth, document.documentElement.clientWidth], lh }; }"""
            )
            shot = os.environ.get("UI4_SHOT_DIR")
            if shot:
                page.screenshot(path=f"{shot}/proof-{theme}-{width}.png")
            assert got["w"][0] <= got["w"][1], got
            assert len(got["figs"]) == 2 and got["pill"], got
    
            def hit(a: list[float], b: list[float]) -> bool:
                return a[0] < b[2] and b[0] < a[2] and a[1] < b[3] and b[1] < a[3]
    
            if got["pill"]:
                assert not any(hit(got["pill"], f) for f in got["figs"]), got  # the pill never covers a figure
            assert max(got["labels"]) <= 3 * 20, got  # a label is at most 3 lines, not one word per line
            if width >= 1280:
                assert got["h"] <= 170, got  # one calm row, not a 230 px tall block
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=theme)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("route", ["overview", "leads", "needs", "log", "data"])
    def test_every_visible_text_has_aa_contrast(env: Any, route: str, theme: str) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        ids = _seed_long(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + f"/ui/#/{route}")
            page.wait_for_timeout(900)
            _set(page, theme, "0")
            page.wait_for_timeout(300)
            found = page.evaluate(_CONTRAST_JS)
            if route == "leads":
                page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{ids[0]}"]')
                page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="lead-drawer"]')
                page.wait_for_timeout(500)
                found += page.evaluate(_CONTRAST_JS)
            low = [f for f in found if f["ratio"] < 4.5]
            assert not low, low[:12]

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
______ test_needs_cards_with_their_lead_number_have_aa_contrast[light-1] _______

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49c3fb90>, theme = 'light', hide = '1'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("hide", ["1", "0"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_needs_cards_with_their_lead_number_have_aa_contrast(env: Any, theme: str, hide: str) -> None:
        """The small lead number on a Needs card (the way to tell two masked cards apart) and the card's other texts."""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_names(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/needs")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="need-lead-number"]')
            _set(page, theme, hide)
            # (a button on a gradient has no single background colour: its white text is checked against every stop below)
            low = [f for f in page.evaluate(_CONTRAST_JS) if f["ratio"] < 4.5 and "btn-primary" not in f["cls"]]
            assert not low, low[:12]
            stops = page.evaluate(
                "() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.btn-primary:not([disabled])')]"
                r".flatMap((b) => getComputedStyle(b).backgroundImage.match(/rgb\([^)]*\)/g) || [])"
            )
            assert stops, "the primary button has no gradient stops to measure"
            for stop in stops:
                channels = [int(v) / 255 for v in stop[4:-1].split(",")[:3]]
                lin = [c / 12.92 if c <= 0.03928 else ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4 for c in channels]
                lum = 0.2126 * lin[0] + 0.7152 * lin[1] + 0.0722 * lin[2]
                assert 1.05 / (lum + 0.05) >= 4.5, (stop, "white text on this stop is below 4.5:1")
    
>       _browse(
            base, body, width=1280, scheme=theme, init=f"window.sessionStorage.setItem('lead-desk-hide-personal', '{hide}')"
        )

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49c989b0>, theme = 'dark', hide = '1'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("hide", ["1", "0"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_needs_cards_with_their_lead_number_have_aa_contrast(env: Any, theme: str, hide: str) -> None:
        """The small lead number on a Needs card (the way to tell two masked cards apart) and the card's other texts."""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_names(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/needs")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="need-lead-number"]')
            _set(page, theme, hide)
            # (a button on a gradient has no single background colour: its white text is checked against every stop below)
            low = [f for f in page.evaluate(_CONTRAST_JS) if f["ratio"] < 4.5 and "btn-primary" not in f["cls"]]
            assert not low, low[:12]
            stops = page.evaluate(
                "() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.btn-primary:not([disabled])')]"
                r".flatMap((b) => getComputedStyle(b).backgroundImage.match(/rgb\([^)]*\)/g) || [])"
            )
            assert stops, "the primary button has no gradient stops to measure"
            for stop in stops:
                channels = [int(v) / 255 for v in stop[4:-1].split(",")[:3]]
                lin = [c / 12.92 if c <= 0.03928 else ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4 for c in channels]
                lum = 0.2126 * lin[0] + 0.7152 * lin[1] + 0.0722 * lin[2]
                assert 1.05 / (lum + 0.05) >= 4.5, (stop, "white text on this stop is below 4.5:1")
    
>       _browse(
            base, body, width=1280, scheme=theme, init=f"window.sessionStorage.setItem('lead-desk-hide-personal', '{hide}')"
        )

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_____ test_masked_values_and_their_eye_buttons_do_not_overlap[1100-light] ______

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49cc2b10>, theme = 'light', width = 1100

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1280, 1300])
    def test_masked_values_and_their_eye_buttons_do_not_overlap(env: Any, theme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_long(rig)
        rig.desk.handle("form", "s-0300", lead(name=UNBROKEN_NAME, email="unbroken." + "x" * 60 + "@example.test"))
    
        def check(page: Any, where: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
            got = page.evaluate(_BUTTON_FACTS_JS)
            assert len(got) >= 8, (where, got)  # a name and an address in each of four rows
            assert all(g["gap"] >= 0 for g in got), (where, got)  # the button starts after the text ends
            assert all(g["inside"] and g["clickable"] for g in got), (where, got)  # and is neither clipped nor covered
            assert not any(g["painted_under"] for g in got), (where, got)  # and no text runs on beneath it
            # the rules behind that, read from the computed layout (a deleted rule in leads.css fails here)
            assert all(g["guard_display"] == "flex" and g["guard_min_width"] == "0px" for g in got), (where, got)
            assert all(g["host_display"] == "flex" and g["host_overflow"] == "visible" for g in got), (where, got)
            return list(got)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id] .lead-cell .eye-btn")
            _set(page, theme, "1")
            check(page, "masked")
            # every value shown on purpose: the long ones are cut by their own ellipsis, and the button still sits clear
            # of the text, inside the cell, and can be clicked
            page.evaluate(
                "() => document.querySelectorAll('.lead-cell [data-guard-key]').forEach("
                "(b) => Portal.reveal.toggle(b.getAttribute('data-guard-key')))"
            )
            page.wait_for_function("() => document.querySelectorAll('.lead-cell .guard .is-masked').length === 0")
            shown = check(page, "shown")
            assert any(g["cut"] and g["text"] > 40 for g in shown), shown  # the very long unbroken values were really cut
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=theme)

            check(page, "masked")
            # every value shown on purpose: the long ones are cut by their own ellipsis, and the button still sits clear
            # of the text, inside the cell, and can be clicked
            page.evaluate(
                "() => document.querySelectorAll('.lead-cell [data-guard-key]').forEach("
                "(b) => Portal.reveal.toggle(b.getAttribute('data-guard-key')))"
            )
            page.wait_for_function("() => document.querySelectorAll('.lead-cell .guard .is-masked').length === 0")
            shown = check(page, "shown")
            assert any(g["cut"] and g["text"] > 40 for g in shown), shown  # the very long unbroken values were really cut
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width, scheme=theme)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1280, 1300])
    def test_masked_values_and_their_eye_buttons_do_not_overlap(env: Any, theme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_long(rig)
        rig.desk.handle("form", "s-0300", lead(name=UNBROKEN_NAME, email="unbroken." + "x" * 60 + "@example.test"))
    
        def check(page: Any, where: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
            got = page.evaluate(_BUTTON_FACTS_JS)
            assert len(got) >= 8, (where, got)  # a name and an address in each of four rows
            assert all(g["gap"] >= 0 for g in got), (where, got)  # the button starts after the text ends
            assert all(g["inside"] and g["clickable"] for g in got), (where, got)  # and is neither clipped nor covered
            assert not any(g["painted_under"] for g in got), (where, got)  # and no text runs on beneath it
            # the rules behind that, read from the computed layout (a deleted rule in leads.css fails here)
            assert all(g["guard_display"] == "flex" and g["guard_min_width"] == "0px" for g in got), (where, got)
            assert all(g["host_display"] == "flex" and g["host_overflow"] == "visible" for g in got), (where, got)
            return list(got)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id] .lead-cell .eye-btn")
            _set(page, theme, "1")
            check(page, "masked")
            # every value shown on purpose: the long ones are cut by their own ellipsis, and the button still sits clear
            # of the text, inside the cell, and can be clicked
            page.evaluate(
                "() => document.querySelectorAll('.lead-cell [data-guard-key]').forEach("
                "(b) => Portal.reveal.toggle(b.getAttribute('data-guard-key')))"
            )
            page.wait_for_function("() => document.querySelectorAll('.lead-cell .guard .is-masked').length === 0")
            shown = check(page, "shown")
            assert any(g["cut"] and g["text"] > 40 for g in shown), shown  # the very long unbroken values were really cut

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_ test_a_name_like_request_number_is_never_in_a_masked_page_and_its_eye_shows_it[light] _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49c3f260>, theme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("theme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_a_name_like_request_number_is_never_in_a_masked_page_and_its_eye_shows_it(env: Any, theme: str) -> None:
        """A request number is data the sender chose, so it is masked like a name on EVERY page and panel (Overview, Leads
        table and drawer, later-message card, Needs, Request log, Data); the page text and the markup (tooltips, labels,
        row attributes) hold no part of it. One eye shows exactly ONE request number (the others stay masked), in the
        drawer ledger, in a later-message card and in the Request log."""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        lid = str(
            rig.desk.handle("form", REF_NAME_LIKE, lead(name="Ada Sample", email="ada.sample@example.test")).body["lead_id"]
        )
        rig.clock.advance(5)
        rig.desk.handle(
            "webhook",
            REF_NAME_LIKE + "-b",
            lead(name="Ada Sample", email="ada.sample@example.test", phone="555 0199", message="later"),
        )
        rig.clock.advance(5)
        rig.desk.handle(
            "form", REF_NAME_LIKE + "-r", {"name": "Ada Sample"}
        )  # no address: a request for a person to look at
        DOTS = "•" * 6
    
        def clean(page: Any, where: str) -> None:
            html = page.content().lower()
            text = page.inner_text("body").lower()
            assert "lovelace" not in html and "lovelace" not in text, where
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            # every page, masked: nothing of the request number anywhere in the text or the markup
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="feed-item"]')
            clean(page, "overview")
            assert any(DOTS in (t or "") for t in page.eval_on_selector_all(".ov-text", "(els) => els.map((e) => e.title)"))
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/needs")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="needs-card"]')
            clean(page, "needs")
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/data")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="data-hide-chip"]')
            clean(page, "data")
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector(f'[data-lead-id="{lid}"]')
            clean(page, "leads table")
            page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{lid}"]')
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="proof-ledger"]')
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="later-card"]')
            clean(page, "lead drawer")
            ledger = page.locator('[data-testid="proof-ledger"] .ledger-step').filter(has_text="Request number")
            later = page.locator('[data-testid="later-card"] .later-meta').first
            assert DOTS in ledger.inner_text() and DOTS in later.inner_text()  # both masked
            # ONE eye in the drawer ledger: that request number shows (short form), the later message's stays masked
            ledger.locator(".eye-btn").click()
            ledger.filter(has_text="ace-42").wait_for()
            assert DOTS in later.inner_text() and "e-42-b" not in later.inner_text()
            # ONE eye in the later-message card: that card shows its own short form
            later.locator(".eye-btn").click()
            later.filter(has_text="e-42-b").wait_for()
            assert "lovelace" not in page.content().lower()  # the short form only (an ellipsis and the last six characters)
            page.keyboard.press("Escape")  # hides every single reveal
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/log")
            page.wait_for_selector(".log-req .eye-btn")
            clean(page, "request log")
            assert page.locator(".log-req .eye-btn").count() == 3
            oldest = page.locator(".log-req").last  # newest first: the first request sent is the last row
            newest = page.locator(".log-req").first
            oldest.locator(".eye-btn").click()  # one value shown on purpose
            oldest.filter(has_text="ace-42").wait_for()
            assert DOTS in newest.inner_text() and "e-42-r" not in newest.inner_text()  # the others stay masked
            assert "lovelace" not in page.content().lower()
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=1280, scheme=theme)

______ test_request_number_masking_is_in_the_shared_rule_not_in_one_page _______

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49b7a960>

    def test_request_number_masking_is_in_the_shared_rule_not_in_one_page(env: Any) -> None:
        """The rule lives in the shared code every page calls (Portal.shownRef, shortRef, requestRef), so no page can
        forget it: while the switch is on EVERY request number (plain, address-like, long) is six dots; with it off the
        number shows as it is, or as an ellipsis and its last six characters."""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        dots = "•" * 6
        long_ref = "x" * 30
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_function("() => window.Portal && Portal.shownRef && Portal.ui && Portal.ui.requestRef")
            probe = (
                "() => ['ui-1', 'ada-lovelace-42', 'a@example.test', 'x'.repeat(30)].map((r) => Portal.shownRef(r))"
                ".concat([Portal.ui.shortRef('x'.repeat(30)), Portal.ui.requestRef('ada-lovelace-42')])"
            )
            page.evaluate("() => Portal.privacy.set(true)")
            assert page.evaluate(probe) == [dots] * 5 + ["Request " + dots]
            page.evaluate("() => Portal.privacy.set(false)")
            assert page.evaluate(probe) == [
                "ui-1",
                "ada-lovelace-42",
                "a@example.test",
                long_ref,
                "…xxxxxx",
                "Request …ace-42",
            ]
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=1280)

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d4ada0650>, scheme = 'light', width = 1280

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1280, 1100, 390])
    def test_a_long_unbroken_name_stays_inside_the_lead_panel_header(env: Any, scheme: str, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        lead_id = _new(rig, "long-1", LONG_NAME, "long@example.test")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            page.locator('[data-testid="leads-hide-personal"]').uncheck()  # names shown: the long text is on screen
            page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{lead_id}"]')
            page.wait_for_selector(HEAD + " h2")
            assert LONG_NAME in page.inner_text(HEAD + " h2")
            page.wait_for_function(STOPPED)
            m = page.evaluate(MEASURE)
            assert m["scroll"] <= m["client"] + 1, m  # the header does not run out of its own box
            assert m["close"][2] >= 35, m  # the close button keeps its size (a squeezed button is 32 wide or less)
            left, right = m["box"][0] - 1, min(m["box"][1], m["vw"]) + 1
            for part in ("h2", "pill", "close"):  # every part lies inside the panel and the window (left side too)
                assert left <= m[part][0] and m[part][1] <= right, (part, m)
            first_line = m["h2"][3] + 36  # a title of many lines: the chip and the close button stay beside its first line
            assert m["pill"][3] < first_line and m["close"][3] < first_line, m
            page.click('[data-testid="drawer-close"]')  # and it can be clicked
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="lead-drawer"]', state="detached")

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [390, 700])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", ["light", "dark"])
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("page_name", list(PAGES))
    def test_no_page_is_wider_than_a_phone_window_and_every_box_lies_inside_it(
        env: Any, scheme: str, width: int, page_name: str
    ) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_names(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open(page, base, page_name)
            m = page.evaluate(BOXES)
            assert m["page"] <= m["window"], m  # no sideways scroll of the page (a wide table scrolls inside its card)
            assert m["checked"] >= 10, m  # the check looked at something
            assert not m["out"], m  # and no box (a card, a tile, a search box) lies beyond a window edge

_ test_the_rail_is_icon_only_up_to_860_px_and_the_page_margin_follows[390-64] __

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49c084a0>, width = 390, rail = 64

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width, rail", [(390, 64), (700, 64), (860, 64), (861, 208), (1200, 208), (1201, 240)])
    def test_the_rail_is_icon_only_up_to_860_px_and_the_page_margin_follows(env: Any, width: int, rail: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_selector("#main")
            m = page.evaluate(
                """() => ({ rail: parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.shell')).gridTemplateColumns),
                  margin: parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.page')).paddingLeft) })"""
            )
            assert m["rail"] == rail, m
            if width <= 860:
                assert m["margin"] == 16, m  # the 860 px page margin (16 px), not the 24 px of the 1200 px rule
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width)

                assert m["margin"] == 16, m  # the 860 px page margin (16 px), not the 24 px of the 1200 px rule

____ test_a_window_with_a_half_pixel_falls_into_a_size_range_too[860.5-208] ____

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49a7b4a0>, width = 860.5, rail = 208

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width, rail", [(860.5, 208), (1200.5, 240)])
    def test_a_window_with_a_half_pixel_falls_into_a_size_range_too(env: Any, width: float, rail: int) -> None:
        """A page cannot be 860.5 px wide in a browser window, but a frame can: its media queries use its own width.
        (Ranges written as min-width 861 and max-width 860 left such a width in neither block: the wide rail won.)"""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            def no_embed_ban(
                route: Any,
            ) -> None:  # the desk forbids frames (CSP); this test page is the only one that allows it
                headers = {
                    k: v
                    for k, v in route.fetch().headers.items()
                    if k not in ("content-security-policy", "x-frame-options")
                }
                route.fulfill(response=route.fetch(), headers=headers)
    
            page.route("**/ui/", no_embed_ban)
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/overview")
            page.wait_for_selector("#main")
            page.evaluate(
                """(w) => { const f = document.createElement('iframe');
                  f.style.cssText = `position:fixed;top:0;left:0;height:800px;width:${w}px;border:0`;
                  f.src = '/ui/#/leads'; document.body.appendChild(f); return new Promise((r) => { f.onload = r; }); }""",
                width,
            )
            frame = page.frames[-1]
            frame.wait_for_selector("#main")
            m = frame.evaluate(
                """() => ({ rail: parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.shell')).gridTemplateColumns),
                  wide: matchMedia('(width > 860px)').matches, narrow: matchMedia('(max-width: 860px)').matches })"""
            )
            assert m["rail"] == rail, m
            assert m["wide"] != m["narrow"], m  # the two ranges are complements: exactly one holds
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=1400)

_ test_the_tabs_do_not_wrap_and_nothing_of_the_active_tab_is_clipped_at_390_px _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49a9a150>

    def test_the_tabs_do_not_wrap_and_nothing_of_the_active_tab_is_clipped_at_390_px(env: Any) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open(page, base, "needs")
            page.keyboard.press("Tab")  # the next focus counts as a keyboard focus (a visible ring)
            page.evaluate("() => document.querySelector('.tabs .tab').focus()")
            m = page.evaluate(
                """() => {
                  const tabs = document.querySelector('.tabs'), first = document.querySelector('.tabs .tab');
                  const t = tabs.getBoundingClientRect(), r = first.getBoundingClientRect(), cs = getComputedStyle(first);
                  const grow = Math.max(0, parseFloat(cs.outlineOffset) + parseFloat(cs.outlineWidth));
                  const glow = (cs.boxShadow.match(/(\\d+)px\\s*$/) || [0, 0])[1];  // the spread of the ring's glow
                  const halo = Number(glow);
                  const room = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(tabs).paddingBottom);
                  return { height: r.height,
                           wrap: cs.whiteSpace, ringTop: r.top - grow - halo - t.top,
                           ringLeft: r.left - grow - halo - t.left,
                           ringBottom: t.bottom - 1 - (r.bottom + grow + halo), underlineRoom: room };
                }"""
            )
            assert m["wrap"] == "nowrap" and m["height"] < 60, m  # the first tab is not three lines high
            assert m["ringTop"] >= 0 and m["ringLeft"] >= 0, m  # the focus ring is not cut off at the top or the left
            assert m["underlineRoom"] >= 1, (
                m
            )  # the 2 px underline of the active tab reaches 1 px below the tab: room for it
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=390)

________ test_the_lead_panel_header_masks_the_name_and_follows_the_eye _________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49cf1eb0>

    def test_the_lead_panel_header_masks_the_name_and_follows_the_eye(env: Any) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        first = _seed_names(rig)

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_ test_a_shown_state_does_not_outlive_the_session_and_old_local_storage_is_ignored _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49ce2a50>

    def test_a_shown_state_does_not_outlive_the_session_and_old_local_storage_is_ignored(env: Any) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_names(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            assert NAME not in page.content()
            page.evaluate(f"() => {{ window.localStorage.setItem('{SEEN_KEY}', '0'); }}")  # what the old build stored
            page.reload()
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            assert NAME not in page.content()  # a stale shown state in local storage changes nothing
            page.uncheck('[data-testid="leads-hide-personal"]')
            assert NAME in page.inner_text('[data-testid="leads-table"]')
            assert page.evaluate(f"() => window.sessionStorage.getItem('{SEEN_KEY}')") == "0"
            assert page.evaluate(f"() => window.localStorage.getItem('{SEEN_KEY}')") == "0"  # untouched, never read
            page.context.clear_cookies()
            fresh_page = page.context.browser.new_context().new_page()  # a new session: nothing carried over
            fresh_page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            fresh_page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            assert NAME not in fresh_page.content()
    
>       _browse(base, body)

_ test_leads_table_shows_owner_and_step_inside_the_card_and_source_hides_up_to_1300[1100] _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49c96f00>, width = 1100

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("width", [1100, 1280, 1366, 1440, 1920])
    def test_leads_table_shows_owner_and_step_inside_the_card_and_source_hides_up_to_1300(env: Any, width: int) -> None:
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_names(rig)
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            head = page.inner_text('[data-testid="leads-table"] thead')
            assert "Lead owner" in head and "Step" in head and "Last change" in head
            m = page.evaluate(
                "() => { const w = document.querySelector('.leads-out .table-wrap');"
                " const th = [...document.querySelectorAll('.leads-out thead th')].map("
                "x => [x.textContent.trim(), x.getBoundingClientRect().right, getComputedStyle(x).display]);"
                " return {sw: w.scrollWidth, cw: w.clientWidth, th: th,"
                " page: document.documentElement.scrollWidth, vw: window.innerWidth}; }"
            )
            assert m["sw"] <= m["cw"] + 1, m  # no sideways scroll inside the card: "Last change" stays on it
            assert m["page"] <= m["vw"], m
            source = [x for x in m["th"] if x[0] == "Source"][0]
            assert (source[2] == "none") == (width <= 1300), (width, source)  # the room is used as soon as there is some
            last = [x for x in m["th"] if x[0] == "Last change"][0]
            assert last[1] <= m["vw"], m
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=width)

        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{first}"]')
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="step-select"]')
            opts = page.eval_on_selector_all('[data-testid="step-select"] option', "o => o.map(x => x.value)")
            assert opts[0] == "" and opts[1] == "contacted" and "new" not in opts  # only later steps are offered
            assert page.is_disabled('[data-testid="step-move"]')
            page.select_option('[data-testid="step-select"]', "replied")
            page.click('[data-testid="step-move"]')
            # the poll redraws the panel, so the element can be missing for a moment: wait on the text
            page.wait_for_function(
                "() => document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"step-now\"]')?.textContent === 'Replied'", timeout=15000
            )
            opts = page.eval_on_selector_all('[data-testid="step-select"] option', "o => o.map(x => x.value)")
            assert "contacted" not in opts and "replied" not in opts and "qualified" in opts

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
_ test_the_panel_fills_even_when_the_lead_answer_comes_after_the_old_panel_closed _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49c0b4a0>

    def test_the_panel_fills_even_when_the_lead_answer_comes_after_the_old_panel_closed(env: Any) -> None:
        """Moving a lead redraws the open panel: the old panel is closed (its 'close' event comes a moment later) and a
        new one opens. If the answer for the new panel is slow, the old panel's close handler used to clear the new
        panel's state, so it stayed on its loading bars for good (a 1 in 10 failure of the step test on Linux)."""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        first = _seed_names(rig)
        slow = {"on": False}
    
        def late(route: Any) -> None:
            if slow["on"] and route.request.method == "GET":
                got = route.fetch()
                time.sleep(0.6)  # fault injection: the answer arrives after the old panel's close event
                route.fulfill(response=got)
            else:
                route.continue_()
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.route("**/api/ui/leads/L*", late)
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{first}"]')
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="step-select"]')
            page.select_option('[data-testid="step-select"]', "replied")
            slow["on"] = True
            page.click('[data-testid="step-move"]')
            page.wait_for_function(
                "() => document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"step-now\"]')?.textContent === 'Replied'", timeout=15000
            )
    
>       _browse(base, body)

../q5-block/playwright/__init__.py:1: ImportError
___ test_the_sidebar_column_is_one_colour_to_the_page_bottom_at_1100[light] ____

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49a39340>, scheme = 'light'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_the_sidebar_column_is_one_colour_to_the_page_bottom_at_1100(env: Any, scheme: str) -> None:
        """At 1200 px and below the strip under the sidebar was the page colour, not the sidebar's: a white block under
        the sidebar when the page is taller than the window. Measured on a full-page screenshot."""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        for i in range(12):
            _new(rig, f"s-{i}", f"Sidney Hart{i}", f"s{i}@example.test")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            import base64
    
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            page.wait_for_timeout(500)
            height = page.evaluate("() => document.documentElement.scrollHeight")
            assert height > 900, height  # the page really is taller than the window
            assert height - 40 > 900, ("second sample point is inside the rail", height)  # the rail is 900 px high
            # A third point, on the brand mark (a fixed element in the rail's header): its colour is far from the rail's,
            # so a reader that returned one value for every point would be caught below.
            box = page.locator(".mark").first.bounding_box()
            assert box is not None
            mark_pt = [round(box["x"]) + 4, round(box["y"] + box["height"] / 2)]
            # The browser reads the screenshot's pixels itself (no image library): a blank page in the same browser
            # decodes the PNG onto a canvas. A blank page, because the portal page's own rules could refuse a data: image.
            shot = "data:image/png;base64," + base64.b64encode(page.screenshot(full_page=True)).decode()
            reader = page.context.new_page()
            try:
                got = reader.evaluate(
                    """async ([src, points]) => {
                        const img = new Image();
                        img.src = src;
                        await img.decode();
                        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
                        canvas.width = img.naturalWidth;
                        canvas.height = img.naturalHeight;
                        const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
                        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
                        return {size: [canvas.width, canvas.height],
                                px: points.map(([x, y]) => Array.from(ctx.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data))};
                    }""",
                    [shot, [[60, 880], [60, height - 40], mark_pt]],
                )
            finally:
                reader.close()
            assert got["size"][1] > height - 40, got["size"]  # both sample points lie inside the screenshot
            above, below, mark = got["px"]
            assert max(abs(a - b) for a, b in zip(above[:3], mark[:3], strict=True)) > 60, (
                "the reader returns the same colour for different points",
                above,
                mark,
            )
            assert above[3] == below[3] == 255, ("not opaque", above, below)  # opaque: the PNG's own colours
            assert all(abs(a - b) <= 6 for a, b in zip(above[:3], below[:3], strict=True)), (
                "sidebar and strip differ",
                above,
                below,
            )
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=1100, scheme=scheme)

                )
            finally:
                reader.close()
            assert got["size"][1] > height - 40, got["size"]  # both sample points lie inside the screenshot
            above, below, mark = got["px"]
            assert max(abs(a - b) for a, b in zip(above[:3], mark[:3], strict=True)) > 60, (
                "the reader returns the same colour for different points",
                above,
                mark,
            )
            assert above[3] == below[3] == 255, ("not opaque", above, below)  # opaque: the PNG's own colours
            assert all(abs(a - b) <= 6 for a, b in zip(above[:3], below[:3], strict=True)), (
                "sidebar and strip differ",
                above,
                below,
            )
    
>       _browse(base, body, width=1100, scheme=scheme)

________________________ test_name_search_needs_reveal _________________________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49be4fe0>

    def test_name_search_needs_reveal(env: Any) -> None:
        """A person must not be able to probe whether a name exists while names are masked: the page sends by_email=0,
        and the desk then matches only company and lead number. The placeholder says so."""
        rig, base = _flow_rig(env)
        _seed_names(rig)
        assert get(base, "/api/ui/leads?q=Wenda&by_email=0")[1]["total"] == 0
        assert get(base, "/api/ui/leads?q=Wenda&by_email=1")[1]["total"] >= 1
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            assert "name" not in (page.get_attribute('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "placeholder") or "").lower()
            assert "after you turn off hiding" in page.inner_text('[data-testid="leads-search-hint"]')
            page.fill('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "Wenda")
            page.wait_for_selector("text=No leads match")  # the probe finds nothing: nothing to learn about the name
            assert NAME not in page.content()
            page.uncheck('[data-testid="leads-hide-personal"]')
            assert "name" in (page.get_attribute('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "placeholder") or "").lower()
            page.fill('[data-testid="leads-search"]', "Wenda")
            page.wait_for_function("() => document.querySelectorAll('[data-lead-id]').length >= 1")
            assert NAME in page.inner_text('[data-testid="leads-table"]')
    
>       _browse(base, body)

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49c093a0>
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x7f0d49a77860>

    def test_demo_pick_lists_neutral(env: Any, monkeypatch: Any) -> None:
        from test_portal_demo import KeyedCrm, make, wired
    
        from lead_desk import server
    
        srv, furl = env.fakes("blind", "keyed")
        rig = wired(env, furl, crm_cls=KeyedCrm, leadflow=_flow())
        rig.clock.t = int(time.time())  # wall-clock: the desk stamps its own calendar time
        demo = make(rig.desk)
        monkeypatch.setattr(server, "load_demo_portal", lambda desk: demo)
        base = env.serve(rig)
        demo.desk_url = base
        names = [p["name"] for p in demo._people]
        lid = _new(rig, "dm-1", names[0], "dm-person@example.com")
        sql(rig, "UPDATE followups SET state='failed', last_error='refused', updated_at=? WHERE lead_id=?", (5, lid))
    
        def texts(page: Any, sel: str) -> list[str]:
            return page.eval_on_selector_all(f"{sel} option", "o => o.map(x => x.textContent)")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/demo")
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="demo-person"]')
            page.wait_for_function(
                "() => document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=\"demo-retry-item\"] option').length >= 1"
            )
            shown = texts(page, '[data-testid="demo-person"]')
            assert shown[:2] == ["Lead 1", "Lead 2"] and len(shown) == len(names)
            assert not any(n in page.content() for n in names)  # neither pick list holds a name
            page.evaluate("() => Portal.privacy.set(false)")  # names shown
            page.wait_for_timeout(200)
            assert texts(page, '[data-testid="demo-person"]')[0].startswith(names[0])
            assert names[0] in texts(page, '[data-testid="demo-retry-item"]')[0]
            page.evaluate("() => Portal.privacy.set(true)")  # hidden again: relabelled at once, selection kept
            page.wait_for_timeout(200)
            assert texts(page, '[data-testid="demo-person"]')[0] == "Lead 1"
            assert texts(page, '[data-testid="demo-retry-item"]')[0].startswith("Lead 1: ")  # not a row of dots
            assert not any(n in page.content() for n in names)
            page.select_option('[data-testid="demo-person"]', "2")
            page.click('[data-testid="demo-send"]')  # the result line must not name the lead either
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="demo-burst-result"]:not([hidden])')
            assert "Lead 2" in page.inner_text('[data-testid="demo-burst-result"]')  # the result line names no lead
            assert not any(n in page.content() for n in names)
    
>       _browse(base, body)

______ test_step_and_approval_buttons_wake_up_when_the_desk_answers_again ______

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49a26f90>

    def test_step_and_approval_buttons_wake_up_when_the_desk_answers_again(env: Any) -> None:
        """The Move, Approve and Deny buttons are disabled while the desk does not answer, and must be enabled again when it
        answers (a chosen step or approver is still chosen), or a button stays dead after the desk returns. Behaviour in a
        browser: the desk's answers are cut, the buttons go dead, the answers come back, the buttons wake up."""
        rig, base, lid, rid = _approval_rig(env)
        pattern = "**/api/ui/**"
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/#/leads")
            page.wait_for_selector("[data-lead-id]")
            page.click(f'[data-lead-id="{lid}"]')
            page.wait_for_selector('[data-testid="approval"]')
            page.select_option('[data-testid="step-select"]', "replied")
            page.select_option('[data-testid="approver-select"]', "appr-02")
            for button in ("step-move", "approve-button", "deny-button"):
                assert page.is_enabled(f'[data-testid="{button}"]'), button
            page.route(pattern, lambda r: r.abort())  # the desk stops answering
            page.wait_for_function("() => Portal.stale()", timeout=30000)
            for button in ("step-move", "approve-button", "deny-button"):
                page.wait_for_selector(f'[data-testid="{button}"][disabled]', timeout=30000)
            page.unroute(pattern)  # the desk answers again
            page.wait_for_function("() => !Portal.stale()", timeout=30000)
            for button in ("step-move", "approve-button", "deny-button"):
                page.wait_for_selector(f'[data-testid="{button}"]:not([disabled])', timeout=30000)
    
>       _browse(base, body)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("scheme", ["light", "dark"])
    def test_the_real_services_banner_is_warm_and_the_demo_banner_is_not(env: Any, scheme: str) -> None:
        _, real_base = fresh(env, crm_kind="hubspot")
        _, demo_base = fresh(env)
        js = (
            "() => { const t = document.createElement('span'); t.style.color = 'var(--warn-fg)';"
            " document.body.appendChild(t);"
            " const warn = getComputedStyle(t).color; t.remove();"
            " const tag = document.querySelector('#demo-line .tag');"
            " return { cls: document.querySelector('#demo-line').className, tag: getComputedStyle(tag).color, warn,"
            " text: document.querySelector('#demo-line').innerText }; }"
        )
        seen: dict[str, Any] = {}
    
        def look(name: str) -> Any:
            def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
                page.goto(base + "/ui/")
                page.wait_for_selector("#demo-line .tag", state="attached")  # the demo tools' own notice may be hidden
                seen[name] = page.evaluate(js)
    
            return body
    
>       _browse(real_base, look("real"), scheme=scheme)

_______________ test_a_real_crm_stage_move_needs_a_second_click ________________

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49a24f20>

    def test_a_real_crm_stage_move_needs_a_second_click(env: Any) -> None:
        rig, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            page.select_option('[data-testid="step-select"]', "replied")
            page.click('[data-testid="step-move"]')
            assert page.inner_text('[data-testid="step-move"]') == "Yes, write to the CRM"
            page.wait_for_timeout(400)
            seen = page.evaluate(
                "() => { const r = document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"step-crm-warning\"]').getBoundingClientRect();"
                " return [r.top, r.bottom, window.innerHeight]; }"
            )
            assert 0 <= seen[0] and seen[1] <= seen[2], seen  # the warning is on screen when the second question is asked
            page.wait_for_timeout(300)
            assert rig.query("SELECT step FROM lead_state WHERE lead_id=?", (lid,))[0][0] == "new"  # nothing written yet
            page.click('[data-testid="step-move"]')
            page.wait_for_function(_STEP_IS_REPLIED, timeout=15000)
            assert "copy to the CRM is pending" in page.inner_text("body")
    
>       _browse(base, body)

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d49190860>

    def test_the_rendered_stage_warning_names_the_automatic_writes_and_says_may(env: Any) -> None:
        """Behaviour, not a window of source text: the warning as the person reads it, inside the stage control."""
        _, base, lid = _lead_and_open(env, "hubspot")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            _open_panel(page, base, lid)
            warn = page.locator('[data-testid="step-control"] [data-testid="step-crm-warning"]')
            text = warn.inner_text().lower()
            assert "automation" in text and "automatic first step" in text and "after the follow-up" in text, text
            assert "may run" in text and "will run" not in text, text
            assert "note-warn" in warn.get_attribute("class") and warn.locator("svg").count() == 1
    
>       _browse(base, body)

_ test_the_real_services_tone_goes_when_the_desk_comes_back_with_the_demo_tools _

env = <conftest.Env object at 0x7f0d491f3110>

    def test_the_real_services_tone_goes_when_the_desk_comes_back_with_the_demo_tools(env: Any) -> None:
        """A page open on a desk with a real CRM, then the desk restarts with the stand-ins and the demo tools: the amber
        real-services style must not stay on the demo line."""
        _, base = fresh(env, crm_kind="hubspot")
    
        def body(page: Any, base: str) -> None:
            page.goto(base + "/ui/")
            page.wait_for_selector("#demo-line.is-real")
    
            def as_demo(route: Any) -> None:
                reply = route.fetch()
                data = reply.json()
                data["mode"] = {"demo_tools": True, "sample_data": True, "real_services": []}
                route.fulfill(response=reply, body=json.dumps(data))
    
            page.route("**/api/ui/summary*", as_demo)
            page.wait_for_function(
                "() => !document.querySelector('#demo-line').classList.contains('is-real')", timeout=20000
            )
    
>       _browse(base, body)
